Appendix A
Flow equations of the gluon and ghost self-energies in Yang-Mills theory at finite temperature

A.1
Feynman rules

First of all, we present the Feynman rules for relevant propagators and vertices at finite temperature and densities.

A.1.1
Gluon propagator

The kinetic term for the gluon field in
Equation

50
in momentum space is given by

\displaystyle\Gamma_{k,2A}\equiv

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int\frac{d^{4}q}{(2\pi)^{4}}A^{a}_{\mu}(-q)q^{2}\Big[\big(Z_{A,k}^{\mathrm{M}}(q)\Pi_{\mu\nu}^{\mathrm{M}}(q)

\displaystyle+Z_{A,k}^{\mathrm{E}}(q)\Pi_{\mu\nu}^{\mathrm{E}}(q)\big)+\frac{1}{\xi}\Pi_{\mu\nu}^{\parallel}(q)\Big]A^{a}_{\nu}(q)\,,

(322)

where the magnetic projection operator reads

\displaystyle\Pi_{\mu\nu}^{\mathrm{M}}(q)=

\displaystyle(1-\delta_{\mu 0})(1-\delta_{\nu 0})\Big(\delta_{\mu\nu}-\frac{q_{\mu}q_{\nu}}{\bm{q}^{2}}\Big)\,,

(323)

and the electric projection operator

\displaystyle\Pi_{\mu\nu}^{\mathrm{E}}(q)=

\displaystyle\Pi_{\mu\nu}^{\perp}(q)-\Pi_{\mu\nu}^{\mathrm{M}}(q)\,,

(324)

with the transverse and longitudinal tensors given by

\displaystyle\Pi_{\mu\nu}^{\perp}(q)=

\displaystyle\delta_{\mu\nu}-\frac{q_{\mu}q_{\nu}}{q^{2}},\quad\mathrm{and}\quad\Pi_{\mu\nu}^{\parallel}(q)=\frac{q_{\mu}q_{\nu}}{q^{2}}\,,

(325)

respectively. Note that in
Equation

322
different magnetic and electric gluonic dressing functions, i.e.,
Z_{A,k}^{\mathrm{M}}(q)\neq Z_{A,k}^{\mathrm{E}}(q)
, are assumed at finite temperature. Differentiating
Equation

322
w.r.t. the gluon field twice, one obtains

\displaystyle\big(\Gamma_{k,2A}^{(2)AA}\big)^{ab}_{\mu\nu}(q^{\prime},q)\equiv\frac{\delta^{2}\Gamma_{k,2A}}{\delta A^{a}_{\mu}(q^{\prime})\delta A^{b}_{\nu}(q)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle q^{2}\Big[\big(Z_{A,k}^{\mathrm{M}}(q)\Pi_{\mu\nu}^{\mathrm{M}}(q)+Z_{A,k}^{\mathrm{E}}(q)\Pi_{\mu\nu}^{\mathrm{E}}(q)\big)+\frac{1}{\xi}\Pi_{\mu\nu}^{\parallel}(q)\Big]

\displaystyle\times\delta^{ab}(2\pi)^{4}\delta^{4}(q^{\prime}+q)\,.

(326)

Moreover, the regulator for the gluon reads

\displaystyle\big(R_{k}^{AA}\big)^{ab}_{\mu\nu}(q^{\prime},q)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big[\big(Z_{A,k}^{\mathrm{M}}q^{2}r_{B}(q^{2}/k^{2})\Pi_{\mu\nu}^{\mathrm{M}}(q)+Z_{A,k}^{\mathrm{E}}q^{2}r_{B}(q^{2}/k^{2})\Pi_{\mu\nu}^{\mathrm{E}}(q)\big)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\xi}q^{2}r_{B}(q^{2}/k^{2})\Pi_{\mu\nu}^{\parallel}(q)\Big]\delta^{ab}(2\pi)^{4}\delta^{4}(q^{\prime}+q)\,,

(327)

where
Z_{A,k}^{\mathrm{M}}
and
Z_{A,k}^{\mathrm{E}}
are independent of momentum
q
by contrast to those in
Equation

326
. The threshold function
r_{B}(x)
can be chosen to be that in
Equation

10
or
Equation

12
. Therefore, the gluon propagator is readily obtained from
Equation

22
, as follows

\displaystyle\big(G_{k}^{AA}\big)^{ab}_{\mu\nu}(q,q^{\prime})

\displaystyle=(2\pi)^{4}\delta^{4}(q^{\prime}+q)\big(G_{k}^{AA}\big)^{ab}_{\mu\nu}(q)\,,

(328)

with

\displaystyle\big(G_{k}^{AA}\big)^{ab}_{\mu\nu}(q)

\displaystyle=\Big(G_{A,k}^{\mathrm{M}}(q)\Pi_{\mu\nu}^{\mathrm{M}}(q)+G_{A,k}^{\mathrm{E}}(q)\Pi_{\mu\nu}^{\mathrm{E}}(q)\Big)\delta^{ab}\,,

(329)

where one has

\displaystyle G_{A,k}^{\mathrm{M}}(q)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{q^{2}\big[Z_{A,k}^{\mathrm{M}}(q)+Z_{A,k}^{\mathrm{M}}r_{B}(q^{2}/k^{2})\big]}\,,

(330)

\displaystyle G_{A,k}^{\mathrm{E}}(q)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{q^{2}\big[Z_{A,k}^{\mathrm{E}}(q)+Z_{A,k}^{\mathrm{E}}r_{B}(q^{2}/k^{2})\big]}\,.

(331)

Here the gauge parameter is chosen to be
\xi=0
.

A.1.2
Ghost propagator

In the same way the kinetic term for the ghost field in
Equation

50
reads

\displaystyle\Gamma_{k,2c}

\displaystyle\equiv\int\frac{d^{4}q}{(2\pi)^{4}}Z_{c,k}(q)\bar{c}^{a}(-q)q^{2}c^{a}(q)\,,

(332)

where we have used the convention of Fourier transformation for the Grassmann fields as follows

\displaystyle\bar{c}^{a}(x)=

\displaystyle\int\frac{d^{4}q}{(2\pi)^{4}}\bar{c}^{a}(q)e^{iqx}\,,

(333)

\displaystyle c^{a}(x)=

\displaystyle\int\frac{d^{4}q}{(2\pi)^{4}}c^{a}(q)e^{iqx}\,.

(334)

Then, it follows that

\displaystyle\big(\Gamma_{k,2c}^{(2)\bar{c}c}\big)^{ab}(q^{\prime},q)\equiv\frac{\overrightarrow{\delta}}{\delta\bar{c}^{a}(q^{\prime})}\Gamma_{k,2c}\frac{\overleftarrow{\delta}}{\delta c^{b}(q)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle Z_{c,k}(q)q^{2}\delta^{ab}(2\pi)^{4}\delta^{4}(q^{\prime}+q)\,,

(335)

and

\displaystyle\big(\Gamma_{k,2c}^{(2)c\bar{c}}\big)^{ab}(q^{\prime},q)\equiv\frac{\overrightarrow{\delta}}{\delta c^{a}(q^{\prime})}\Gamma_{k,2c}\frac{\overleftarrow{\delta}}{\delta\bar{c}^{b}(q)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-Z_{c,k}(q^{\prime}){q^{\prime}}^{2}\delta^{ab}(2\pi)^{4}\delta^{4}(q^{\prime}+q)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\big(\Gamma_{k,2c}^{(2)\bar{c}c}\big)^{ba}(q,q^{\prime})=-\Big[\big(\Gamma_{k,2c}^{(2)\bar{c}c}\big)^{T}\Big]^{ab}(q^{\prime},q)\,,

(336)

Note that, for a Grassmann field, the relation such as

\displaystyle\Gamma_{k,2c}^{(2)c\bar{c}}

\displaystyle=-\big(\Gamma_{k,2c}^{(2)\bar{c}c}\big)^{T}\,,

(337)

always holds. The regulator for the ghost field reads

\displaystyle\big(R_{k}^{\bar{c}c}\big)^{ab}(q^{\prime},q)

\displaystyle=Z_{c,k}q^{2}r_{B}(q^{2}/k^{2})\delta^{ab}(2\pi)^{4}\delta^{4}(q^{\prime}+q)\,,

(338)

and
R_{k}^{c\bar{c}}=-\big(R_{k}^{\bar{c}c}\big)^{T}
. As a consequence, the
\mathcal{P}
matrix in
Equation

60
for the ghost field reads

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}_{c}

\displaystyle=\begin{pmatrix}0&-(\Gamma_{k,2c}^{(2)\bar{c}c}+R_{k}^{\bar{c}c})^{T}\\
\Gamma_{k,2c}^{(2)\bar{c}c}+R_{k}^{\bar{c}c}&0\end{pmatrix}\,,

(339)

whose inverse matrix is just the ghost propagator which is given by

\displaystyle\left(\frac{1}{\mathcal{P}}\right)_{c}

\displaystyle=\begin{pmatrix}0&G_{k}^{c\bar{c}}\\
-(G_{k}^{c\bar{c}})^{T}&0\end{pmatrix}\,,

(340)

with

\displaystyle G_{k}^{c\bar{c}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\Gamma_{k,2c}^{(2)\bar{c}c}+R_{k}^{\bar{c}c}}\,.

(341)

Recovering the indices, one obtains the ghost propagator as follows

\displaystyle\big(G_{k}^{c\bar{c}}\big)^{ab}(q,q^{\prime})

\displaystyle=\big(G_{k}^{c\bar{c}}\big)^{ab}(q)(2\pi)^{4}\delta^{4}(q+q^{\prime})

\displaystyle=G_{k}^{c}(q)\delta^{ab}(2\pi)^{4}\delta^{4}(q+q^{\prime})\,,

(342)

with

\displaystyle G_{k}^{c}(q)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{q^{2}\big[Z_{c,k}(q)+Z_{c,k}r_{B}(q^{2}/k^{2})\big]}\,.

(343)

A.1.3
Quark propagator

The kinetic term for the quark field in
Equation

50
reads

\displaystyle\Gamma_{k,2q}

\displaystyle\equiv\int\frac{d^{4}q}{(2\pi)^{4}}\bar{q}(-q)\Big[Z_{q,k}(q)i\gamma\cdot q+m_{q,k}(q)\Big]q(q)\,.

(344)

It follows that

\displaystyle\big(\Gamma_{k,2q}^{(2)\bar{q}q}\big)_{ij}(q^{\prime},q)=\frac{\overrightarrow{\delta}}{\delta\bar{q}_{i}(q^{\prime})}\Gamma_{k,2q}\frac{\overleftarrow{\delta}}{\delta q_{j}(q)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big[Z_{q,k}(q)iq_{\mu}(\gamma_{\mu})_{ij}+m_{q,k}(q)\delta_{ij}\Big](2\pi)^{4}\delta^{4}(q^{\prime}+q)\,,

(345)

and

\displaystyle\big(\Gamma_{k,2q}^{(2)q\bar{q}}\big)_{ij}(q^{\prime},q)=\frac{\overrightarrow{\delta}}{\delta q_{i}(q^{\prime})}\Gamma_{k,2q}\frac{\overleftarrow{\delta}}{\delta\bar{q}_{j}(q)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\Big[Z_{q,k}(q^{\prime})iq^{\prime}_{\mu}(\gamma_{\mu})_{ji}+m_{q,k}(q^{\prime})\delta_{ji}\Big](2\pi)^{4}\delta^{4}(q+q^{\prime})

\displaystyle=-\Big[\big(Z_{q,k}(q^{\prime})iq^{\prime}_{\mu}\gamma_{\mu}+m_{q,k}(q^{\prime})\big)^{T}\Big]_{ij}(2\pi)^{4}\delta^{4}(q+q^{\prime})

\displaystyle=-\Big[\big(\Gamma_{k,2q}^{(2)\bar{q}q}\big)^{T}\Big]_{ij}(q^{\prime},q)\,.

(346)

The regulator reads

\displaystyle\big(R_{k}^{\bar{q}q}\big)_{ij}(q^{\prime},q)

\displaystyle=Z_{q,k}iq\cdot\gamma_{ij}r_{F}(q^{2}/k^{2})(2\pi)^{4}\delta^{4}(q^{\prime}+q)\,.

(347)

The
\mathcal{P}
matrix for the quark field reads

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}_{q}

\displaystyle=\begin{pmatrix}0&-(\Gamma_{k,2q}^{(2)\bar{q}q}+R_{k}^{\bar{q}q})^{T}\\
\Gamma_{k,2q}^{(2)\bar{q}q}+R_{k}^{\bar{q}q}&0\end{pmatrix}\,,

(348)

and the relevant propagator is given by

\displaystyle\left(\frac{1}{\mathcal{P}}\right)_{q}

\displaystyle=\begin{pmatrix}0&G_{k}^{q\bar{q}}\\
-(G_{k}^{q\bar{q}})^{T}&0\end{pmatrix}\,,

(349)

with

\displaystyle G_{k}^{q\bar{q}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\Gamma_{k,2q}^{(2)\bar{q}q}+R_{k}^{\bar{q}q}}\,.

(350)

Displaying the momentum explicitly, one arrives at the quark propagator as follows

\displaystyle G_{k}^{q\bar{q}}(q,q^{\prime})

\displaystyle=G_{k}^{q\bar{q}}(q)(2\pi)^{4}\delta^{4}(q+q^{\prime})

\displaystyle=G_{k}^{q}(q)(2\pi)^{4}\delta^{4}(q+q^{\prime})\,,

(351)

with

\displaystyle G^{q}_{k}(q)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{Z_{q,k}(q)i\gamma\cdot q+Z_{q,k}r_{F}(q^{2}/k^{2})i\gamma\cdot q+m_{q,k}(q)}\,.

(352)

A.1.4
Ghost-gluon vertex

First of all, we consider the case in the vacuum. The action relevant to the ghost-gluon interaction in
Equation

50
reads

\displaystyle\Gamma_{k,2cA}\equiv\int_{x}(-g)f^{abc}\partial_{\mu}\bar{c}^{a}(x)c^{b}(x)A^{c}_{\mu}(x)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int\frac{d^{4}p}{(2\pi)^{4}}\frac{d^{4}q}{(2\pi)^{4}}(-g)f^{abc}ip_{\mu}\bar{c}^{a}(p)c^{b}(q)A^{c}_{\mu}(-p-q)\,,

(353)

which yields

\displaystyle\big(\Gamma_{k,2cA}^{(3)\bar{c}cA}\big)^{abc}_{\mu}(p,q,k)\equiv\frac{\delta}{\delta A^{c}_{\mu}(k)}\frac{\overrightarrow{\delta}}{\delta\bar{c}^{a}(p)}\Gamma_{k,2cA}\frac{\overleftarrow{\delta}}{\delta c^{b}(q)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-gf^{abc}ip_{\mu}(2\pi)^{4}\delta^{4}(p+q+k)\,.

(354)

Thus, the Feynman rule for the ghost-gluon vertex in the vacuum is given as follows

\displaystyle\parbox[c]{41.39832pt}{\includegraphics[width]{ghost-gluon-vert}}\equiv-\big(\Gamma_{k,2cA}^{(3)\bar{c}cA}\big)^{abc}_{\mu}(p,q,k)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-g\big(S^{(3)}_{\bar{c}cA}\big)^{abc}_{\mu}(p,q,k)\,.

(355)

with the classical tensor of the ghost-gluon vertex defined by

\displaystyle\big(S^{(3)}_{\bar{c}cA}\big)^{abc}_{\mu}(p,q,k)

\displaystyle\equiv-ip_{\mu}f^{abc}\,,

(356)

At finite temperature, the external leg of gluon would be split into the magnetic and electric sectors, and thus the general form for the ghost-gluon vertex at finite temperature reads

\displaystyle=-\big(S^{(3)}_{\bar{c}cA}\big)^{abc}_{\mu^{\prime}}(p,q,k)\big(\lambda_{\bar{c}cA}\big)_{\mu^{\prime}\mu}(k)\,.

(357)

with

\displaystyle\big(\lambda_{\bar{c}cA}\big)_{\mu^{\prime}\mu}(k)

\displaystyle\equiv\lambda^{\mathrm{M}}_{\bar{c}cA}\Pi_{\mu^{\prime}\mu}^{\mathrm{M}}(k)+\lambda^{\mathrm{E}}_{\bar{c}cA}\Pi_{\mu^{\prime}\mu}^{\mathrm{E}}(k)\,,

(358)

where
\lambda^{\mathrm{M}}_{\bar{c}cA}
and
\lambda^{\mathrm{E}}_{\bar{c}cA}
are the dressing couplings of the ghost-gluon vertex for the magnetic and electric components, respectively. Note that the couplings are dependent on the RG scale, and their subscript
k
has been suppressed.

A.1.5
Three- and four-gluon vertices

From the effective action in
Equation

50
, one is able to obtain the three-gluon vertex in the vacuum, as follows

\displaystyle\big(\Gamma_{k}^{(3)A^{3}}\big)^{a_{1}a_{2}a_{3}}_{\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}}(q_{1},q_{2},q_{3})

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\frac{\delta^{3}\Gamma_{k}}{\delta A^{a_{1}}_{\mu_{1}}(q_{1})\delta A^{a_{2}}_{\mu_{2}}(q_{2})\delta A^{a_{3}}_{\mu_{3}}(q_{3})}\bigg|_{A=0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(2\pi)^{4}\delta^{4}(q_{1}+q_{2}+q_{3})g\big(S^{(3)}_{A^{3}}\big)^{a_{1}a_{2}a_{3}}_{\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}}(q_{1},q_{2},q_{3})\,,

(359)

with the classical three-gluon tensor

\displaystyle\big(S^{(3)}_{A^{3}}\big)^{a_{1}a_{2}a_{3}}_{\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}}(q_{1},q_{2},q_{3})

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle-if^{a_{1}a_{2}a_{3}}\Big[\delta_{\mu_{1}\mu_{2}}(q_{1}-q_{2})_{\mu_{3}}+\delta_{\mu_{2}\mu_{3}}(q_{2}-q_{3})_{\mu_{1}}

\displaystyle+\delta_{\mu_{3}\mu_{1}}(q_{3}-q_{1})_{\mu_{2}}\Big]\,.

(360)

In the same way, the four-gluon vertex reads

\displaystyle\big(\Gamma_{k}^{(4)A^{4}}\big)^{a_{1}a_{2}a_{3}a_{4}}_{\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}\mu_{4}}(q_{1},q_{2},q_{3},q_{4})

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\frac{\delta^{4}\Gamma_{k,A}}{\delta A^{a_{1}}_{\mu_{1}}(q_{1})\delta A^{a_{2}}_{\mu_{2}}(q_{2})\delta A^{a_{3}}_{\mu_{3}}(q_{3})\delta A^{a_{4}}_{\mu_{4}}(q_{4})}\bigg|_{A=0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(2\pi)^{4}\delta^{4}(q_{1}+q_{2}+q_{3}+q_{4})g^{2}\big(S^{(4)}_{A^{4}}\big)^{a_{1}a_{2}a_{3}a_{4}}_{\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}\mu_{4}}\,,

(361)

with the classical four-gluon tensor

\displaystyle\big(S^{(4)}_{A^{4}}\big)^{a_{1}a_{2}a_{3}a_{4}}_{\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}\mu_{4}}

\displaystyle\equiv f^{ea_{1}a_{2}}f^{ea_{3}a_{4}}(\delta_{\mu_{1}\mu_{3}}\delta_{\mu_{2}\mu_{4}}-\delta_{\mu_{1}\mu_{4}}\delta_{\mu_{2}\mu_{3}})

\displaystyle+f^{ea_{1}a_{3}}f^{ea_{2}a_{4}}(\delta_{\mu_{1}\mu_{2}}\delta_{\mu_{3}\mu_{4}}-\delta_{\mu_{1}\mu_{4}}\delta_{\mu_{2}\mu_{3}})

\displaystyle+f^{ea_{1}a_{4}}f^{ea_{2}a_{3}}(\delta_{\mu_{1}\mu_{2}}\delta_{\mu_{4}\mu_{3}}-\delta_{\mu_{1}\mu_{3}}\delta_{\mu_{4}\mu_{2}})\,.

(362)

Similar with the case of ghost-gluon vertex, each external leg of the three- and four-gluon vertices should be split into a sum of the magnetic and electric components at finite temperature, and see, e.g.,
Cyrol et al. 2018a
for more details. Consequently, the three-gluon vertex at finite temperature reads

\displaystyle\parbox[c]{41.39832pt}{\includegraphics[width]{A3-vert}}\equiv-\big(\Gamma_{k}^{(3)A^{3}}\big)^{a_{1}a_{2}a_{3}}_{\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}}(q_{1},q_{2},q_{3})

\displaystyle=-\big(S^{(3)}_{A^{3}}\big)^{a_{1}a_{2}a_{3}}_{\mu_{1}^{\prime}\mu_{2}^{\prime}\mu_{3}^{\prime}}(q_{1},q_{2},q_{3})\big(\lambda_{A^{3}}\big)^{\mu_{1}^{\prime}\mu_{2}^{\prime}\mu_{3}^{\prime}}_{\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}}(q_{1},q_{2},q_{3})\,,

(363)

with

\displaystyle\big(\lambda_{A^{3}}\big)^{\mu_{1}^{\prime}\mu_{2}^{\prime}\mu_{3}^{\prime}}_{\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}}(q_{1},q_{2},q_{3})

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\lambda^{\mathrm{MMM}}_{A^{3}}\big(\Pi^{\mathrm{MMM}}\big)^{\mu_{1}^{\prime}\mu_{2}^{\prime}\mu_{3}^{\prime}}_{\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}}(q_{1},q_{2},q_{3})

\displaystyle+\lambda^{\mathrm{EMM}}_{A^{3}}\big(\Pi^{\mathrm{EMM}}\big)^{\mu_{1}^{\prime}\mu_{2}^{\prime}\mu_{3}^{\prime}}_{\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}}(q_{1},q_{2},q_{3})

\displaystyle+\lambda^{\mathrm{EEM}}_{A^{3}}\big(\Pi^{\mathrm{EEM}}\big)^{\mu_{1}^{\prime}\mu_{2}^{\prime}\mu_{3}^{\prime}}_{\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}}(q_{1},q_{2},q_{3})

\displaystyle+\lambda^{\mathrm{EEE}}_{A^{3}}\big(\Pi^{\mathrm{EEE}}\big)^{\mu_{1}^{\prime}\mu_{2}^{\prime}\mu_{3}^{\prime}}_{\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}}(q_{1},q_{2},q_{3})\,,

(364)

where
\lambda^{\mathrm{MMM}}_{A^{3}}
,
\lambda^{\mathrm{EMM}}_{A^{3}}
,
\lambda^{\mathrm{EEM}}_{A^{3}}
,
\lambda^{\mathrm{EEE}}_{A^{3}}
are the dressing three-gluon couplings for different components, and the relevant projectors reads

\displaystyle\big(\Pi^{\mathrm{MMM}}\big)^{\mu_{1}^{\prime}\mu_{2}^{\prime}\mu_{3}^{\prime}}_{\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}}(q_{1},q_{2},q_{3})\equiv\Pi_{\mu_{1}^{\prime}\mu_{1}}^{\mathrm{M}}(q_{1})\Pi_{\mu_{2}^{\prime}\mu_{2}}^{\mathrm{M}}(q_{2})\Pi_{\mu_{3}^{\prime}\mu_{3}}^{\mathrm{M}}(q_{3})\,,

(365)

\displaystyle\big(\Pi^{\mathrm{EEE}}\big)^{\mu_{1}^{\prime}\mu_{2}^{\prime}\mu_{3}^{\prime}}_{\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}}(q_{1},q_{2},q_{3})\equiv\Pi_{\mu_{1}^{\prime}\mu_{1}}^{\mathrm{E}}(q_{1})\Pi_{\mu_{2}^{\prime}\mu_{2}}^{\mathrm{E}}(q_{2})\Pi_{\mu_{3}^{\prime}\mu_{3}}^{\mathrm{E}}(q_{3})\,,

(366)

the projector with one electric gluon and two magnetic gluons

\displaystyle\big(\Pi^{\mathrm{EMM}}\big)^{\mu_{1}^{\prime}\mu_{2}^{\prime}\mu_{3}^{\prime}}_{\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}}(q_{1},q_{2},q_{3})

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\Pi_{\mu_{1}^{\prime}\mu_{1}}^{\mathrm{E}}(q_{1})\Pi_{\mu_{2}^{\prime}\mu_{2}}^{\mathrm{M}}(q_{2})\Pi_{\mu_{3}^{\prime}\mu_{3}}^{\mathrm{M}}(q_{3})+\Pi_{\mu_{1}^{\prime}\mu_{1}}^{\mathrm{M}}(q_{1})\Pi_{\mu_{2}^{\prime}\mu_{2}}^{\mathrm{E}}(q_{2})

\displaystyle\times\Pi_{\mu_{3}^{\prime}\mu_{3}}^{\mathrm{M}}(q_{3})+\Pi_{\mu_{1}^{\prime}\mu_{1}}^{\mathrm{M}}(q_{1})\Pi_{\mu_{2}^{\prime}\mu_{2}}^{\mathrm{M}}(q_{2})\Pi_{\mu_{3}^{\prime}\mu_{3}}^{\mathrm{E}}(q_{3})\,,

(367)

the projector with two electric gluons and one magnetic gluon

\displaystyle\big(\Pi^{\mathrm{EEM}}\big)^{\mu_{1}^{\prime}\mu_{2}^{\prime}\mu_{3}^{\prime}}_{\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}}(q_{1},q_{2},q_{3})

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\Pi_{\mu_{1}^{\prime}\mu_{1}}^{\mathrm{E}}(q_{1})\Pi_{\mu_{2}^{\prime}\mu_{2}}^{\mathrm{E}}(q_{2})\Pi_{\mu_{3}^{\prime}\mu_{3}}^{\mathrm{M}}(q_{3})+\Pi_{\mu_{1}^{\prime}\mu_{1}}^{\mathrm{E}}(q_{1})\Pi_{\mu_{2}^{\prime}\mu_{2}}^{\mathrm{M}}(q_{2})

\displaystyle\times\Pi_{\mu_{3}^{\prime}\mu_{3}}^{\mathrm{E}}(q_{3})+\Pi_{\mu_{1}^{\prime}\mu_{1}}^{\mathrm{M}}(q_{1})\Pi_{\mu_{2}^{\prime}\mu_{2}}^{\mathrm{E}}(q_{2})\Pi_{\mu_{3}^{\prime}\mu_{3}}^{\mathrm{E}}(q_{3})\,.

(368)

The four-gluon vertex at finite temperature reads

\displaystyle\parbox[c]{41.39832pt}{\includegraphics[width]{A4-vert}}\equiv-\big(\Gamma_{k}^{(4)A^{4}}\big)^{a_{1}a_{2}a_{3}a_{4}}_{\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}\mu_{4}}(q_{1},q_{2},q_{3},q_{4})

\displaystyle=-\big(S^{(4)}_{A^{4}}\big)^{a_{1}a_{2}a_{3}a_{4}}_{\mu_{1}^{\prime}\mu_{2}^{\prime}\mu_{3}^{\prime}\mu_{4}^{\prime}}\big(\lambda_{A^{4}}\big)^{\mu_{1}^{\prime}\mu_{2}^{\prime}\mu_{3}^{\prime}\mu_{4}^{\prime}}_{\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}\mu_{4}}(q_{1},q_{2},q_{3},q_{4})\,,

(369)

with

\displaystyle\big(\lambda_{A^{4}}\big)^{\mu_{1}^{\prime}\mu_{2}^{\prime}\mu_{3}^{\prime}\mu_{4}^{\prime}}_{\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}\mu_{4}}(q_{1},q_{2},q_{3},q_{4})

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\lambda^{\mathrm{MMMM}}_{A^{4}}\big(\Pi^{\mathrm{MMMM}}\big)^{\mu_{1}^{\prime}\mu_{2}^{\prime}\mu_{3}^{\prime}\mu_{4}^{\prime}}_{\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}\mu_{4}}(q_{1},q_{2},q_{3},q_{4})

\displaystyle+\lambda^{\mathrm{EMMM}}_{A^{4}}\big(\Pi^{\mathrm{EMMM}}\big)^{\mu_{1}^{\prime}\mu_{2}^{\prime}\mu_{3}^{\prime}\mu_{4}^{\prime}}_{\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}\mu_{4}}(q_{1},q_{2},q_{3},q_{4})

\displaystyle+\lambda^{\mathrm{EEMM}}_{A^{4}}\big(\Pi^{\mathrm{EEMM}}\big)^{\mu_{1}^{\prime}\mu_{2}^{\prime}\mu_{3}^{\prime}\mu_{4}^{\prime}}_{\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}\mu_{4}}(q_{1},q_{2},q_{3},q_{4})

\displaystyle+\lambda^{\mathrm{EEEM}}_{A^{4}}\big(\Pi^{\mathrm{EEEM}}\big)^{\mu_{1}^{\prime}\mu_{2}^{\prime}\mu_{3}^{\prime}\mu_{4}^{\prime}}_{\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}\mu_{4}}(q_{1},q_{2},q_{3},q_{4})

\displaystyle+\lambda^{\mathrm{EEEE}}_{A^{4}}\big(\Pi^{\mathrm{EEEE}}\big)^{\mu_{1}^{\prime}\mu_{2}^{\prime}\mu_{3}^{\prime}\mu_{4}^{\prime}}_{\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}\mu_{4}}(q_{1},q_{2},q_{3},q_{4})\,,

(370)

where
\lambda^{\mathrm{MMMM}}_{A^{4}}
,
\lambda^{\mathrm{EMMM}}_{A^{4}}
,
\lambda^{\mathrm{EEMM}}_{A^{4}}
,
\lambda^{\mathrm{EEEM}}_{A^{4}}
, and
\lambda^{\mathrm{EEEE}}_{A^{4}}
are the dressing four-gluon couplings for different components, and the relevant projectors reads

\displaystyle\big(\Pi^{\mathrm{MMMM}}\big)^{\mu_{1}^{\prime}\mu_{2}^{\prime}\mu_{3}^{\prime}\mu_{4}^{\prime}}_{\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}\mu_{4}}(q_{1},q_{2},q_{3},q_{4})

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\Pi_{\mu_{1}^{\prime}\mu_{1}}^{\mathrm{M}}(q_{1})\Pi_{\mu_{2}^{\prime}\mu_{2}}^{\mathrm{M}}(q_{2})\Pi_{\mu_{3}^{\prime}\mu_{3}}^{\mathrm{M}}(q_{3})\Pi_{\mu_{4}^{\prime}\mu_{4}}^{\mathrm{M}}(q_{4})\,,

(371)

and

\displaystyle\big(\Pi^{\mathrm{EEEE}}\big)^{\mu_{1}^{\prime}\mu_{2}^{\prime}\mu_{3}^{\prime}\mu_{4}^{\prime}}_{\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}\mu_{4}}(q_{1},q_{2},q_{3},q_{4})

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\Pi_{\mu_{1}^{\prime}\mu_{1}}^{\mathrm{E}}(q_{1})\Pi_{\mu_{2}^{\prime}\mu_{2}}^{\mathrm{E}}(q_{2})\Pi_{\mu_{3}^{\prime}\mu_{3}}^{\mathrm{E}}(q_{3})\Pi_{\mu_{4}^{\prime}\mu_{4}}^{\mathrm{E}}(q_{4})\,,

(372)

the projector with one electric gluon and three magnetic gluons

\displaystyle\big(\Pi^{\mathrm{EMMM}}\big)^{\mu_{1}^{\prime}\mu_{2}^{\prime}\mu_{3}^{\prime}\mu_{4}^{\prime}}_{\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}\mu_{4}}(q_{1},q_{2},q_{3},q_{4})

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\Pi_{\mu_{1}^{\prime}\mu_{1}}^{\mathrm{E}}(q_{1})\Pi_{\mu_{2}^{\prime}\mu_{2}}^{\mathrm{M}}(q_{2})\Pi_{\mu_{3}^{\prime}\mu_{3}}^{\mathrm{M}}(q_{3})\Pi_{\mu_{4}^{\prime}\mu_{4}}^{\mathrm{M}}(q_{4})

\displaystyle+\Pi_{\mu_{1}^{\prime}\mu_{1}}^{\mathrm{M}}(q_{1})\Pi_{\mu_{2}^{\prime}\mu_{2}}^{\mathrm{E}}(q_{2})\Pi_{\mu_{3}^{\prime}\mu_{3}}^{\mathrm{M}}(q_{3})\Pi_{\mu_{4}^{\prime}\mu_{4}}^{\mathrm{M}}(q_{4})

\displaystyle+\Pi_{\mu_{1}^{\prime}\mu_{1}}^{\mathrm{M}}(q_{1})\Pi_{\mu_{2}^{\prime}\mu_{2}}^{\mathrm{M}}(q_{2})\Pi_{\mu_{3}^{\prime}\mu_{3}}^{\mathrm{E}}(q_{3})\Pi_{\mu_{4}^{\prime}\mu_{4}}^{\mathrm{M}}(q_{4})

\displaystyle+\Pi_{\mu_{1}^{\prime}\mu_{1}}^{\mathrm{M}}(q_{1})\Pi_{\mu_{2}^{\prime}\mu_{2}}^{\mathrm{M}}(q_{2})\Pi_{\mu_{3}^{\prime}\mu_{3}}^{\mathrm{M}}(q_{3})\Pi_{\mu_{4}^{\prime}\mu_{4}}^{\mathrm{E}}(q_{4})\,,

(373)

the projector with three electric gluon and one magnetic gluons

\displaystyle\big(\Pi^{\mathrm{EEEM}}\big)^{\mu_{1}^{\prime}\mu_{2}^{\prime}\mu_{3}^{\prime}\mu_{4}^{\prime}}_{\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}\mu_{4}}(q_{1},q_{2},q_{3},q_{4})

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\Pi_{\mu_{1}^{\prime}\mu_{1}}^{\mathrm{E}}(q_{1})\Pi_{\mu_{2}^{\prime}\mu_{2}}^{\mathrm{E}}(q_{2})\Pi_{\mu_{3}^{\prime}\mu_{3}}^{\mathrm{E}}(q_{3})\Pi_{\mu_{4}^{\prime}\mu_{4}}^{\mathrm{M}}(q_{4})

\displaystyle+\Pi_{\mu_{1}^{\prime}\mu_{1}}^{\mathrm{E}}(q_{1})\Pi_{\mu_{2}^{\prime}\mu_{2}}^{\mathrm{E}}(q_{2})\Pi_{\mu_{3}^{\prime}\mu_{3}}^{\mathrm{M}}(q_{3})\Pi_{\mu_{4}^{\prime}\mu_{4}}^{\mathrm{E}}(q_{4})

\displaystyle+\Pi_{\mu_{1}^{\prime}\mu_{1}}^{\mathrm{E}}(q_{1})\Pi_{\mu_{2}^{\prime}\mu_{2}}^{\mathrm{M}}(q_{2})\Pi_{\mu_{3}^{\prime}\mu_{3}}^{\mathrm{E}}(q_{3})\Pi_{\mu_{4}^{\prime}\mu_{4}}^{\mathrm{E}}(q_{4})

\displaystyle+\Pi_{\mu_{1}^{\prime}\mu_{1}}^{\mathrm{M}}(q_{1})\Pi_{\mu_{2}^{\prime}\mu_{2}}^{\mathrm{E}}(q_{2})\Pi_{\mu_{3}^{\prime}\mu_{3}}^{\mathrm{E}}(q_{3})\Pi_{\mu_{4}^{\prime}\mu_{4}}^{\mathrm{E}}(q_{4})\,,

(374)

the projector with two electric gluons and two magnetic gluons

\displaystyle\big(\Pi^{\mathrm{EEMM}}\big)^{\mu_{1}^{\prime}\mu_{2}^{\prime}\mu_{3}^{\prime}\mu_{4}^{\prime}}_{\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}\mu_{4}}(q_{1},q_{2},q_{3},q_{4})

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\Pi_{\mu_{1}^{\prime}\mu_{1}}^{\mathrm{E}}(q_{1})\Pi_{\mu_{2}^{\prime}\mu_{2}}^{\mathrm{E}}(q_{2})\Pi_{\mu_{3}^{\prime}\mu_{3}}^{\mathrm{M}}(q_{3})\Pi_{\mu_{4}^{\prime}\mu_{4}}^{\mathrm{M}}(q_{4})

\displaystyle+\Pi_{\mu_{1}^{\prime}\mu_{1}}^{\mathrm{E}}(q_{1})\Pi_{\mu_{2}^{\prime}\mu_{2}}^{\mathrm{M}}(q_{2})\Pi_{\mu_{3}^{\prime}\mu_{3}}^{\mathrm{E}}(q_{3})\Pi_{\mu_{4}^{\prime}\mu_{4}}^{\mathrm{M}}(q_{4})

\displaystyle+\Pi_{\mu_{1}^{\prime}\mu_{1}}^{\mathrm{E}}(q_{1})\Pi_{\mu_{2}^{\prime}\mu_{2}}^{\mathrm{M}}(q_{2})\Pi_{\mu_{3}^{\prime}\mu_{3}}^{\mathrm{M}}(q_{3})\Pi_{\mu_{4}^{\prime}\mu_{4}}^{\mathrm{E}}(q_{4})

\displaystyle+\Pi_{\mu_{1}^{\prime}\mu_{1}}^{\mathrm{M}}(q_{1})\Pi_{\mu_{2}^{\prime}\mu_{2}}^{\mathrm{E}}(q_{2})\Pi_{\mu_{3}^{\prime}\mu_{3}}^{\mathrm{E}}(q_{3})\Pi_{\mu_{4}^{\prime}\mu_{4}}^{\mathrm{M}}(q_{4})

\displaystyle+\Pi_{\mu_{1}^{\prime}\mu_{1}}^{\mathrm{M}}(q_{1})\Pi_{\mu_{2}^{\prime}\mu_{2}}^{\mathrm{E}}(q_{2})\Pi_{\mu_{3}^{\prime}\mu_{3}}^{\mathrm{M}}(q_{3})\Pi_{\mu_{4}^{\prime}\mu_{4}}^{\mathrm{E}}(q_{4})

\displaystyle+\Pi_{\mu_{1}^{\prime}\mu_{1}}^{\mathrm{M}}(q_{1})\Pi_{\mu_{2}^{\prime}\mu_{2}}^{\mathrm{M}}(q_{2})\Pi_{\mu_{3}^{\prime}\mu_{3}}^{\mathrm{E}}(q_{3})\Pi_{\mu_{4}^{\prime}\mu_{4}}^{\mathrm{E}}(q_{4})\,.

(375)

A.2
Gluon self-energy

With the Feynman rules discussed in
Section

A.1
, it is straightforward to write down expressions for the loop diagrams of the gluon self-energy as shown on the r.h.s. of flow equation in
Figure

3
. The first gluon loop reads

\displaystyle\equiv\big(\Sigma^{AA}_{a}\big)_{\mu\nu}^{ab}(p)\,.

(376)

with

\displaystyle\big(\Sigma^{AA}_{a}\big)_{\mu\nu}^{ab}(p)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int\frac{d^{4}q}{(2\pi)^{4}}(-)\big(S^{(3)}_{A^{3}}\big)^{aa_{1}a_{2}}_{\mu^{\prime}\mu_{1}^{\prime}\mu_{2}^{\prime}}(-p,-q+p,q)

\displaystyle\times\big(\lambda_{A^{3}}\big)^{\mu^{\prime}\mu_{1}^{\prime}\mu_{2}^{\prime}}_{\mu\mu_{1}\mu_{2}}(-p,-q+p,q)\Big[G_{A}^{\mathrm{M}}(q)\Pi_{\mu_{2}\nu_{2}}^{\mathrm{M}}(q)

\displaystyle+G_{A}^{\mathrm{E}}(q)\Pi_{\mu_{2}\nu_{2}}^{\mathrm{E}}(q)\Big]\delta^{a_{2}b_{2}}(-)\big(S^{(3)}_{A^{3}}\big)^{bb_{1}b_{2}}_{\nu^{\prime}\nu_{1}^{\prime}\nu_{2}^{\prime}}(p,q-p,-q)

\displaystyle\times\big(\lambda_{A^{3}}\big)^{\nu^{\prime}\nu_{1}^{\prime}\nu_{2}^{\prime}}_{\nu\nu_{1}\nu_{2}}(p,q-p,-q)\Big[G_{A}^{\mathrm{M}}(q-p)\Pi_{\nu_{1}\mu_{1}}^{\mathrm{M}}(q-p)

\displaystyle+G_{A}^{\mathrm{E}}(q-p)\Pi_{\nu_{1}\mu_{1}}^{\mathrm{E}}(q-p)\Big]\delta^{b_{1}a_{1}}\,.

(377)

Projecting
Equation

377
onto the magnetic component, one arrives at

\displaystyle\big(\Sigma^{AA}_{a}\big)_{\mu\nu}^{ab}(p)\big(\delta^{ab}\Pi_{\mu\nu}^{\mathrm{M}}(p)\big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle N_{c}(N_{c}^{2}-1)\int\frac{d^{4}q}{(2\pi)^{4}}\bigg[{\lambda^{\mathrm{MMM}}_{A^{3}}}^{2}G_{A}^{\mathrm{M}}(q)G_{A}^{\mathrm{M}}(q-p)C^{M}_{MM}

\displaystyle+{\lambda^{\mathrm{EEM}}_{A^{3}}}^{2}G_{A}^{\mathrm{E}}(q)G_{A}^{\mathrm{E}}(q-p)C^{M}_{EE}+{\lambda^{\mathrm{EMM}}_{A^{3}}}^{2}

\displaystyle\times\Big(G_{A}^{\mathrm{M}}(q)G_{A}^{\mathrm{E}}(q-p)C^{M}_{ME}+G_{A}^{\mathrm{E}}(q)G_{A}^{\mathrm{M}}(q-p)C^{M}_{EM}\Big)\bigg]\,.

(378)

Here we have defined several coefficients, which reads

\displaystyle C^{M}_{MM}=

\displaystyle\frac{2\sin^{2}\theta}{p_{s}^{2}+q_{s}^{2}-2p_{s}q_{s}\cos\theta}\bigg\{11p_{s}^{2}q_{s}^{2}+4p_{s}^{4}+4q_{s}^{4}

\displaystyle+p_{s}q_{s}\left[p_{s}q_{s}\cos 2\theta-8\left(p_{s}^{2}+q_{s}^{2}\right)\cos\theta\right]\bigg\}\,,

(379)

where the two 4-momenta are
p=(p_{0},\bm{p})
and
q=(q_{0},\bm{q})
, with
p_{s}=|\bm{p}|
,
q_{s}=|\bm{q}|
and
\cos\theta=\bm{p}\cdot\bm{q}/(p_{s}q_{s})
. In the same way, we have

\displaystyle C^{M}_{ME}=

\displaystyle\frac{2(\cos 2\theta+3)}{p_{s}^{2}+q_{s}^{2}-2p_{s}q_{s}\cos\theta}

\displaystyle\times\frac{\Big[q_{0}p_{s}^{2}+p_{0}q_{s}^{2}-p_{s}q_{s}\left(p_{0}+q_{0}\right)\cos\theta\Big]^{2}}{\left(p_{0}-q_{0}\right)^{2}+p_{s}^{2}+q_{s}^{2}-2p_{s}q_{s}\cos\theta}\,.

(380)

The coefficient
C^{M}_{EM}
could be deduced from
C^{M}_{ME}
through the replacement as follows

\displaystyle C^{M}_{EM}=C^{M}_{ME}\Big|_{q\rightarrow-q+p}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2\left(p_{s}q_{0}\cos\theta-p_{0}q_{s}\right)^{2}}{\left(q_{0}^{2}+q_{s}^{2}\right)\left(p_{s}^{2}-2p_{s}q_{s}\cos\theta+q_{s}^{2}\right)}

\displaystyle\times\bigg[4p_{s}^{2}-8p_{s}q_{s}\cos\theta+(3+\cos 2\theta)q_{s}^{2}\bigg]\,.

(381)

Finally, the last one is given by

\displaystyle C^{M}_{EE}=

\displaystyle\frac{4\sin^{2}\theta}{\left(q_{0}^{2}+q_{s}^{2}\right)\left(p_{s}^{2}-2p_{s}q_{s}\cos\theta+q_{s}^{2}\right)}

\displaystyle\times\frac{1}{p_{s}^{2}+\left(p_{0}-q_{0}\right)^{2}-2p_{s}q_{s}\cos\theta+q_{s}^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\Big\{q_{s}^{4}+p_{s}^{2}q_{0}^{2}+\left(p_{0}^{2}+p_{s}^{2}-p_{0}q_{0}+q_{0}^{2}\right)q_{s}^{2}

\displaystyle-p_{s}q_{s}\left[q_{0}\left(p_{0}+q_{0}\right)+2q_{s}^{2}\right]\cos\theta\Big\}^{2}\,.

(382)

Projection of
Equation

377
onto the electric component yields

\displaystyle\big(\Sigma^{AA}_{a}\big)_{\mu\nu}^{ab}(p)\left(\delta^{ab}\Pi_{\mu\nu}^{\mathrm{E}}(p)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle N_{c}(N_{c}^{2}-1)\int\frac{d^{4}q}{(2\pi)^{4}}\bigg[{\lambda^{\mathrm{EEE}}_{A^{3}}}^{2}G_{A}^{\mathrm{E}}(q)G_{A}^{\mathrm{E}}(q-p)C^{E}_{EE}

\displaystyle+{\lambda^{\mathrm{EMM}}_{A^{3}}}^{2}G_{A}^{\mathrm{M}}(q)G_{A}^{\mathrm{M}}(q-p)C^{E}_{MM}+{\lambda^{\mathrm{EEM}}_{A^{3}}}^{2}

\displaystyle\times\Big(G_{A}^{\mathrm{M}}(q)G_{A}^{\mathrm{E}}(q-p)C^{E}_{ME}+G_{A}^{\mathrm{E}}(q)G_{A}^{\mathrm{M}}(q-p)C^{E}_{EM}\Big)\bigg]\,.

(383)

There are four coefficients as well, and their explicit expressions are given as follows,

\displaystyle C^{E}_{EE}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\left(p_{0}^{2}+p_{s}^{2}\right)\left(q_{0}^{2}+q_{s}^{2}\right)}\frac{1}{p_{s}^{2}-2p_{s}q_{s}\cos\theta+q_{s}^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\frac{1}{p_{s}^{2}+\left(p_{0}-q_{0}\right)^{2}-2p_{s}q_{s}\cos\theta+q_{s}^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\Bigg\{p_{s}q_{s}\bigg[\Big(2p_{s}^{2}q_{0}+p_{0}q_{0}\left(p_{0}+q_{0}\right)+2p_{0}q_{s}^{2}\Big)\cos 2\theta

\displaystyle+\left(p_{0}+q_{0}\right)\Big(2p_{0}^{2}-3p_{0}q_{0}+2\left(p_{s}^{2}+q_{0}^{2}+q_{s}^{2}\right)\Big)\bigg]

\displaystyle-2\bigg[p_{s}^{2}q_{0}\Big(p_{0}^{2}+p_{s}^{2}-p_{0}q_{0}+q_{0}^{2}\Big)+\Big(p_{0}^{3}-p_{0}^{2}q_{0}

\displaystyle+2p_{s}^{2}q_{0}+p_{0}\left(2p_{s}^{2}+q_{0}^{2}\right)\Big)q_{s}^{2}+p_{0}q_{s}^{4}\bigg]\cos\theta\Bigg\}^{2}\,,

(384)

coefficient
C^{E}_{ME}

\displaystyle C^{E}_{ME}=

\displaystyle\frac{4\sin^{2}\theta}{\left(p_{0}^{2}+p_{s}^{2}\right)\left(p_{s}^{2}-2p_{s}q_{s}\cos\theta+q_{s}^{2}\right)}

\displaystyle\times\frac{1}{p_{s}^{2}+\left(p_{0}-q_{0}\right)^{2}-2p_{s}q_{s}\cos\theta+q_{s}^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\bigg[p_{s}^{2}\left(p_{0}^{2}+p_{s}^{2}-p_{0}q_{0}+q_{0}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle-p_{s}q_{s}\Big(2p_{s}^{2}+p_{0}\left(p_{0}+q_{0}\right)\Big)\cos\theta+\left(p_{0}^{2}+p_{s}^{2}\right)q_{s}^{2}\bigg]^{2}\,,

(385)

coefficient
C^{E}_{EM}

\displaystyle C^{E}_{EM}=C^{E}_{ME}\Big|_{q\rightarrow-q+p}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{4\sin^{2}\theta}{\left(p_{0}^{2}+p_{s}^{2}\right)\left(q_{0}^{2}+q_{s}^{2}\right)}\frac{1}{p_{s}^{2}-2p_{s}q_{s}\cos\theta+q_{s}^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\bigg[p_{s}^{2}\left(q_{0}^{2}+q_{s}^{2}\right)+p_{0}^{2}q_{s}^{2}-p_{0}p_{s}q_{0}q_{s}\cos\theta\bigg]^{2}\,,

(386)

and coefficient
C^{E}_{MM}

\displaystyle C^{E}_{MM}=

\displaystyle\frac{2\left(p_{s}q_{0}-p_{0}q_{s}\cos\theta\right)^{2}}{\left(p_{0}^{2}+p_{s}^{2}\right)\left(p_{s}^{2}-2p_{s}q_{s}\cos\theta+q_{s}^{2}\right)}

\displaystyle\times\left(3p_{s}^{2}+p_{s}^{2}\cos 2\theta-8p_{s}q_{s}\cos\theta+4q_{s}^{2}\right)\,.

(387)

The ghost loop of the gluon self-energy, i.e., the second diagram on the r.h.s. of flow equation in
Figure

3
, reads

\displaystyle\equiv\big(\Sigma^{AA}_{b}\big)_{\mu\nu}^{ab}(p)\,.

(388)

with

\displaystyle\big(\Sigma^{AA}_{b}\big)_{\mu\nu}^{ab}(p)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int\frac{d^{4}q}{(2\pi)^{4}}(-)\big(S^{(3)}_{\bar{c}cA}\big)^{a_{1}a_{2}a}_{\mu^{\prime}}(-q+p,q,-p)\big(\lambda_{\bar{c}cA}\big)_{\mu^{\prime}\mu}(-p)

\displaystyle\times G_{c}(q)\delta^{a_{2}b_{2}}(-)\big(S^{(3)}_{\bar{c}cA}\big)^{b_{2}b_{1}b}_{\nu^{\prime}}(-q,q-p,p)\big(\lambda_{\bar{c}cA}\big)_{\nu^{\prime}\nu}(p)

\displaystyle\times G_{c}(q-p)\delta^{b_{1}a_{1}}\,.

(389)

Projecting
Equation

389
onto the magnetic component leads us to

\displaystyle\big(\Sigma^{AA}_{b}\big)_{\mu\nu}^{ab}(p)\left(\delta^{ab}\Pi_{\mu\nu}^{\mathrm{M}}(p)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int\frac{d^{4}q}{(2\pi)^{4}}N_{c}(N_{c}^{2}-1)(\lambda^{\mathrm{M}}_{\bar{c}cA})^{2}G_{c}(q)G_{c}(q-p)C^{g}_{M}\,,

(390)

with

\displaystyle C^{g}_{M}

\displaystyle=q_{s}^{2}\sin^{2}\theta\,.

(391)

And the electric component is given by

\displaystyle\big(\Sigma^{AA}_{b}\big)_{\mu\nu}^{ab}(p)\left(\delta^{ab}\Pi_{\mu\nu}^{\mathrm{E}}(p)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int\frac{d^{4}q}{(2\pi)^{4}}N_{c}(N_{c}^{2}-1)(\lambda^{\mathrm{E}}_{\bar{c}cA})^{2}G_{c}(q)G_{c}(q-p)C^{g}_{E}\,,

(392)

with

\displaystyle C^{g}_{E}

\displaystyle=\frac{\big(p_{s}q_{0}-p_{0}q_{s}\cos\theta\big)^{2}}{p_{0}^{2}+p_{s}^{2}}\,.

(393)

The tadpole diagram of the gluon self-energy due to the four-gluon vertex, i.e., the third diagram on the r.h.s. of flow equation in
Figure

3
, reads

\displaystyle\equiv\big(\Sigma^{AA}_{c}\big)_{\mu\nu}^{ab}(p)\,.

(394)

with

\displaystyle\big(\Sigma^{AA}_{c}\big)_{\mu\nu}^{ab}(p)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int\frac{d^{4}q}{(2\pi)^{4}}(-)\big(S^{(4)}_{A^{4}}\big)^{aa_{1}b_{1}b}_{\mu^{\prime}\mu_{1}^{\prime}\nu_{1}^{\prime}\nu^{\prime}}\big(\lambda_{A^{4}}\big)^{\mu^{\prime}\mu_{1}^{\prime}\nu_{1}^{\prime}\nu^{\prime}}_{\mu\mu_{1}\nu_{1}\nu}(-p,-q,q,p)

\displaystyle\times\Big(G_{A}^{\mathrm{M}}(q)\Pi_{\mu_{1}\nu_{1}}^{\mathrm{M}}(q)+G_{A}^{\mathrm{E}}(q)\Pi_{\mu_{1}\nu_{1}}^{\mathrm{E}}(q)\Big)\delta^{a_{1}b_{1}}\,.

(395)

Projecting
Equation

395
onto the magnetic component leaves us with

\displaystyle\big(\Sigma^{AA}_{c}\big)_{\mu\nu}^{ab}(p)\left(\delta^{ab}\Pi_{\mu\nu}^{\mathrm{M}}(p)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int\frac{d^{4}q}{(2\pi)^{4}}N_{c}(N_{c}^{2}-1)\Big[\lambda^{\mathrm{MMMM}}_{A^{4}}G_{A}^{\mathrm{M}}(q)C^{t}_{MM}

\displaystyle+\lambda^{\mathrm{EEMM}}_{A^{4}}G_{A}^{\mathrm{E}}(q)C^{t}_{ME}\Big]\,,

(396)

with

\displaystyle C^{t}_{MM}

\displaystyle=\cos(2\theta)-5\,,

(397)

\displaystyle C^{t}_{ME}

\displaystyle=-\frac{2\big(q_{0}^{2}+q_{0}^{2}\cos^{2}\theta+2q_{s}^{2}\big)}{q_{0}^{2}+q_{s}^{2}}\,.

(398)

The electric component reads

\displaystyle\big(\Sigma^{AA}_{c}\big)_{\mu\nu}^{ab}(p)\left(\delta^{ab}\Pi_{\mu\nu}^{\mathrm{E}}(p)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int\frac{d^{4}q}{(2\pi)^{4}}N_{c}(N_{c}^{2}-1)\Big[\lambda^{\mathrm{EEMM}}_{A^{4}}G_{A}^{\mathrm{M}}(q)C^{t}_{EM}

\displaystyle+\lambda^{\mathrm{EEEE}}_{A^{4}}G_{A}^{\mathrm{E}}(q)C^{t}_{EE}\Big]\,,

(399)

with

\displaystyle C^{t}_{EM}

\displaystyle=-\frac{2\big(p_{0}^{2}+p_{0}^{2}\cos^{2}\theta+2p_{s}^{2}\big)}{p_{0}^{2}+p_{s}^{2}}\,,

(400)

\displaystyle C^{t}_{EE}

\displaystyle=-\frac{2\left[p_{s}^{2}q_{0}^{2}-2p_{0}p_{s}q_{0}q_{s}\cos\theta+p_{0}^{2}\left(q_{0}^{2}\sin^{2}\theta+q_{s}^{2}\right)\right]}{\left(p_{0}^{2}+p_{s}^{2}\right)\left(q_{0}^{2}+q_{s}^{2}\right)}\,,

(401)

Substituting Eqs. (
378
), (
383
), (
390
), (
392
), (
396
), (
399
) into the flow equation in
Figure

3
, one obtains the flow equation of the magnetic gluon dressing function

\displaystyle\partial_{t}Z_{A,k}^{\mathrm{M}}(p)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{N_{c}}{2p^{2}}\int\frac{d^{4}q}{(2\pi)^{4}}\Bigg\{\bigg[{\lambda^{\mathrm{MMM}}_{A^{3}}}^{2}(\tilde{\partial}_{t}G_{A}^{\mathrm{M}}(q))G_{A}^{\mathrm{M}}(q-p)C^{M}_{MM}

\displaystyle+{\lambda^{\mathrm{EEM}}_{A^{3}}}^{2}(\tilde{\partial}_{t}G_{A}^{\mathrm{E}}(q))G_{A}^{\mathrm{E}}(q-p)C^{M}_{EE}+{\lambda^{\mathrm{EMM}}_{A^{3}}}^{2}\Big((\tilde{\partial}_{t}G_{A}^{\mathrm{M}}(q))

\displaystyle\times G_{A}^{\mathrm{E}}(q-p)C^{M}_{ME}+(\tilde{\partial}_{t}G_{A}^{\mathrm{E}}(q))G_{A}^{\mathrm{M}}(q-p)C^{M}_{EM}\Big)\bigg]

\displaystyle-2(\lambda^{\mathrm{M}}_{\bar{c}cA})^{2}(\tilde{\partial}_{t}G_{c}(q))G_{c}(q-p)C^{g}_{M}+\frac{1}{2}\Big[\lambda^{\mathrm{MMMM}}_{A^{4}}

\displaystyle\times(\tilde{\partial}_{t}G_{A}^{\mathrm{M}}(q))C^{t}_{MM}+\lambda^{\mathrm{EEMM}}_{A^{4}}(\tilde{\partial}_{t}G_{A}^{\mathrm{E}}(q))C^{t}_{ME}\Big]\Bigg\}\,,

(402)

and the flow equation of the electric gluon dressing function

\displaystyle\partial_{t}Z_{A,k}^{\mathrm{E}}(p)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{N_{c}}{p^{2}}\int\frac{d^{4}q}{(2\pi)^{4}}\Bigg\{\bigg[{\lambda^{\mathrm{EEE}}_{A^{3}}}^{2}(\tilde{\partial}_{t}G_{A}^{\mathrm{E}}(q))G_{A}^{\mathrm{E}}(q-p)C^{E}_{EE}

\displaystyle+{\lambda^{\mathrm{EMM}}_{A^{3}}}^{2}(\tilde{\partial}_{t}G_{A}^{\mathrm{M}}(q))G_{A}^{\mathrm{M}}(q-p)C^{E}_{MM}+{\lambda^{\mathrm{EEM}}_{A^{3}}}^{2}\Big((\tilde{\partial}_{t}G_{A}^{\mathrm{M}}(q))

\displaystyle\times G_{A}^{\mathrm{E}}(q-p)C^{E}_{ME}+(\tilde{\partial}_{t}G_{A}^{\mathrm{E}}(q))G_{A}^{\mathrm{M}}(q-p)C^{E}_{EM}\Big)\bigg]

\displaystyle-2(\lambda^{\mathrm{E}}_{\bar{c}cA})^{2}(\tilde{\partial}_{t}G_{c}(q))G_{c}(q-p)C^{g}_{E}+\frac{1}{2}\Big[\lambda^{\mathrm{EEMM}}_{A^{4}}

\displaystyle\times(\tilde{\partial}_{t}G_{A}^{\mathrm{M}}(q))C^{t}_{EM}+\lambda^{\mathrm{EEEE}}_{A^{4}}(\tilde{\partial}_{t}G_{A}^{\mathrm{E}}(q))C^{t}_{EE}\Big]\Bigg\}\,,

(403)

A.3
Ghost self-energy

Figure 59:
Diagrammatic representation of the flow equation for the ghost self-energy in Yang-Mills theory.

The flow equation of the ghost self-energy in Yang-Mills theory is depicted in
Figure

59
. The one-loop diagram on the r.h.s. reads

\displaystyle\equiv\big(\Sigma^{\bar{c}c}\big)^{ab}(p)\,.

(404)

with

\displaystyle\big(\Sigma^{\bar{c}c}\big)^{ab}(p)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int\frac{d^{4}q}{(2\pi)^{4}}(-)\big(S^{(3)}_{\bar{c}cA}\big)^{aa_{1}a_{2}}_{\mu_{2}^{\prime}}(-p,q,-q+p)

\displaystyle\times\big(\lambda_{\bar{c}cA}\big)_{\mu_{2}^{\prime}\mu_{2}}(-q+p)G_{c}(q)\delta^{a_{1}b_{1}}

\displaystyle\times(-)\big(S^{(3)}_{\bar{c}cA}\big)^{b_{1}bb_{2}}_{\nu_{2}^{\prime}}(-q,p,q-p)

\displaystyle\times\big(\lambda_{\bar{c}cA}\big)_{\nu_{2}^{\prime}\nu_{2}}(q-p)\Big[G_{A}^{\mathrm{M}}(q-p)\Pi_{\mu_{2}\nu_{2}}^{\mathrm{M}}(q-p)

\displaystyle+G_{A}^{\mathrm{E}}(q-p)\Pi_{\mu_{2}\nu_{2}}^{\mathrm{E}}(q-p)\Big]\delta^{a_{2}b_{2}}\,.

(405)

Tracing the color indices, one arrives at

\displaystyle\big(\Sigma^{\bar{c}c}\big)^{ab}(p)\delta^{ab}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int\frac{d^{4}q}{(2\pi)^{4}}N_{c}(N_{c}^{2}-1)\Big[(\lambda^{\mathrm{M}}_{\bar{c}cA})^{2}G_{A}^{\mathrm{M}}(q-p)G_{c}(q)C^{\bar{c}c}_{M}

\displaystyle+(\lambda^{\mathrm{E}}_{\bar{c}cA})^{2}G_{A}^{\mathrm{E}}(q-p)G_{c}(q)C^{\bar{c}c}_{E}\Big]\,,

(406)

with

\displaystyle C^{\bar{c}c}_{M}=

\displaystyle\frac{p_{s}^{2}q_{s}^{2}\sin^{2}\theta}{p_{s}^{2}-2p_{s}q_{s}\cos\theta+q_{s}^{2}}\,,

(407)

\displaystyle C^{\bar{c}c}_{E}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\Big(p_{s}^{2}-2p_{s}q_{s}\cos\theta+q_{s}^{2}\Big)}

\displaystyle\times\frac{\Big[p_{s}^{2}q_{0}-p_{s}q_{s}\left(p_{0}+q_{0}\right)\cos\theta+p_{0}q_{s}^{2}\Big]^{2}}{p_{s}^{2}+\left(p_{0}-q_{0}\right)^{2}-2p_{s}q_{s}\cos\theta+q_{s}^{2}}\,.

(408)

In the following we also need the expression in
Equation

406
with the replacement of the internal momentum
q\rightarrow-q+p
, which reads

\displaystyle\big(\Sigma^{\bar{c}c}\big)^{ab}(p)\delta^{ab}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int\frac{d^{4}q}{(2\pi)^{4}}N_{c}(N_{c}^{2}-1)\Big[(\lambda^{\mathrm{M}}_{\bar{c}cA})^{2}G_{A}^{\mathrm{M}}(q)G_{c}(q-p)C^{\prime\bar{c}c}_{M}

\displaystyle+(\lambda^{\mathrm{E}}_{\bar{c}cA})^{2}G_{A}^{\mathrm{E}}(q)G_{c}(q-p)C^{\prime\bar{c}c}_{E}\Big]\,,

(409)

with

\displaystyle C^{\prime\bar{c}c}_{M}

\displaystyle=C^{\bar{c}c}_{M}\Big|_{q\rightarrow-q+p}\,,\quad C^{\prime\bar{c}c}_{E}

\displaystyle=C^{\bar{c}c}_{E}\Big|_{q\rightarrow-q+p}\,.

(410)

Finally, inserting Eqs. (
406
) and (
409
) into the flow equation in
Figure

59
, one is led to the flow equation of the ghost dressing function, as follows

\displaystyle\partial_{t}Z_{c,k}(p)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{N_{c}}{p^{2}}\int\frac{d^{4}q}{(2\pi)^{4}}\Bigg\{\Big[(\lambda^{\mathrm{M}}_{\bar{c}cA})^{2}G_{A}^{\mathrm{M}}(q-p)(\tilde{\partial}_{t}G_{c}(q))C^{\bar{c}c}_{M}

\displaystyle+(\lambda^{\mathrm{E}}_{\bar{c}cA})^{2}G_{A}^{\mathrm{E}}(q-p)(\tilde{\partial}_{t}G_{c}(q))C^{\bar{c}c}_{E}\Big]

\displaystyle+\Big[(\lambda^{\mathrm{M}}_{\bar{c}cA})^{2}(\tilde{\partial}_{t}G_{A}^{\mathrm{M}}(q))G_{c}(q-p)C^{\prime\bar{c}c}_{M}

\displaystyle+(\lambda^{\mathrm{E}}_{\bar{c}cA})^{2}(\tilde{\partial}_{t}G_{A}^{\mathrm{E}}(q))G_{c}(q-p)C^{\prime\bar{c}c}_{E}\Big]\Bigg\}\,.

(411)

Appendix B
Fierz-complete basis of four-quark interactions of
N_{f}=2
flavors

Here we list the Fierz-complete basis of four-quark interactions of
N_{f}=2
flavors
Equation

85
, see also e.g.,
Mitter et al. 2015
;
Cyrol et al. 2018b
;
Fu et al. 2022
. The ten different channels can be classified into four different subsets according to their invariance or not, under the global transformations of the groups
SU_{\mathrm{V}}(N_{f})
,
U_{\mathrm{V}}(1)
,
SU_{\mathrm{A}}(N_{f})
, and
U_{\mathrm{A}}(1)
. Of the ten channels, several are invariant under all the transformations mentioned above, which read

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}^{(V-A)}_{ijlm}{\bar{q}}_{i}q_{j}{\bar{q}}_{l}q_{m}=

\displaystyle(\bar{q}\gamma_{\mu}T^{0}q)^{2}-(\bar{q}i\gamma_{\mu}\gamma_{5}T^{0}q)^{2}\,,

(412)

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}^{(V+A)}_{ijlm}{\bar{q}}_{i}q_{j}{\bar{q}}_{l}q_{m}=

\displaystyle(\bar{q}\gamma_{\mu}T^{0}q)^{2}+(\bar{q}i\gamma_{\mu}\gamma_{5}T^{0}q)^{2}\,,

(413)

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}^{(S-P)_{+}}_{ijlm}{\bar{q}}_{i}q_{j}{\bar{q}}_{l}q_{m}=

\displaystyle(\bar{q}\,T^{0}q)^{2}-(\bar{q}\,\gamma_{5}T^{0}q)^{2}

\displaystyle+(\bar{q}\,T^{a}q)^{2}-(\bar{q}\,\gamma_{5}T^{a}q)^{2}\,,

(414)

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}^{(V-A)^{\mathrm{adj}}}_{ijlm}{\bar{q}}_{i}q_{j}{\bar{q}}_{l}q_{m}=

\displaystyle(\bar{q}\gamma_{\mu}T^{0}t^{a}q)^{2}-(\bar{q}i\gamma_{\mu}\gamma_{5}T^{0}t^{a}q)^{2}\,,

(415)

where generators of the flavor
SU(N_{f})
group and the color
SU(N_{c})
group are denoted by
T^{a}
’s and
t^{a}
’s, respectively, and summation for the indices is assumed. Furthermore, one has
T^{0}=1/\sqrt{2N_{f}}\mathbb{1}_{N_{f}\times N_{f}}
. Another two channels, given by

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}^{(S+P)_{-}}_{ijlm}{\bar{q}}_{i}q_{j}{\bar{q}}_{l}q_{m}=

\displaystyle(\bar{q}\,T^{0}q)^{2}+(\bar{q}\,\gamma_{5}T^{0}q)^{2}

\displaystyle-(\bar{q}\,T^{a}q)^{2}-(\bar{q}\,\gamma_{5}T^{a}q)^{2}\,,

(416)

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}^{(S+P)_{-}^{\mathrm{adj}}}_{ijlm}{\bar{q}}_{i}q_{j}{\bar{q}}_{l}q_{m}=

\displaystyle(\bar{q}\,T^{0}t^{a}q)^{2}+(\bar{q}\,\gamma_{5}T^{0}t^{a}q)^{2}

\displaystyle-(\bar{q}\,T^{a}t^{b}q)^{2}-(\bar{q}\,\gamma_{5}T^{a}t^{b}q)^{2}\,,

(417)

break the symmetry of
U_{\mathrm{A}}(1)
while preserve
SU_{\mathrm{V}}(N_{f})\otimes U_{\mathrm{V}}(1)\otimes SU_{\mathrm{A}}(N_{f})
. The two channels that read

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}^{(S-P)_{-}}_{ijlm}{\bar{q}}_{i}q_{j}{\bar{q}}_{l}q_{m}=

\displaystyle(\bar{q}\,T^{0}q)^{2}-(\bar{q}\,\gamma_{5}T^{0}q)^{2}

\displaystyle-(\bar{q}\,T^{a}q)^{2}+(\bar{q}\,\gamma_{5}T^{a}q)^{2}\,,

(418)

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}^{(S-P)_{-}^{\mathrm{adj}}}_{ijlm}{\bar{q}}_{i}q_{j}{\bar{q}}_{l}q_{m}=

\displaystyle(\bar{q}\,T^{0}t^{a}q)^{2}-(\bar{q}\,\gamma_{5}T^{0}t^{a}q)^{2}

\displaystyle-(\bar{q}\,T^{a}t^{b}q)^{2}+(\bar{q}\,\gamma_{5}T^{a}t^{b}q)^{2}\,,

(419)

break
SU_{\mathrm{A}}(N_{f})
while preserve
SU_{\mathrm{V}}(N_{f})\otimes U_{\mathrm{V}}(1)\otimes U_{\mathrm{A}}(1)
. The last two independent channels, viz.,

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}^{(S+P)_{+}}_{ijlm}{\bar{q}}_{i}q_{j}{\bar{q}}_{l}q_{m}=

\displaystyle(\bar{q}\,T^{0}q)^{2}+(\bar{q}\,\gamma_{5}T^{0}q)^{2}

\displaystyle+(\bar{q}\,T^{a}q)^{2}+(\bar{q}\,\gamma_{5}T^{a}q)^{2}\,,

(420)

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}^{(S+P)_{+}^{\mathrm{adj}}}_{ijlm}{\bar{q}}_{i}q_{j}{\bar{q}}_{l}q_{m}=

\displaystyle(\bar{q}\,T^{0}t^{a}q)^{2}+(\bar{q}\,\gamma_{5}T^{0}t^{a}q)^{2}

\displaystyle+(\bar{q}\,T^{a}t^{b}q)^{2}+(\bar{q}\,\gamma_{5}T^{a}t^{b}q)^{2}\,.

(421)

break both
U_{\mathrm{A}}(1)
and
SU_{\mathrm{A}}(N_{f})
, while preserve
SU_{\mathrm{V}}(N_{f})\otimes U_{\mathrm{V}}(1)
.

Moreover, it is also useful to combine linearly several different channels of the four-quark couplings to form new independent elements of basis. For instance, the four scalar-pseudoscalar channels as follows

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}^{\sigma}_{ijlm}{\bar{q}}_{i}q_{j}{\bar{q}}_{l}q_{m}=

\displaystyle(\bar{q}\,T^{0}q)^{2}\,,

(422)

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}^{\pi}_{ijlm}{\bar{q}}_{i}q_{j}{\bar{q}}_{l}q_{m}=

\displaystyle-(\bar{q}\,\gamma_{5}T^{a}q)^{2}\,,

(423)

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}^{a}_{ijlm}{\bar{q}}_{i}q_{j}{\bar{q}}_{l}q_{m}=

\displaystyle(\bar{q}\,T^{a}q)^{2}\,,

(424)

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}^{\eta}_{ijlm}{\bar{q}}_{i}q_{j}{\bar{q}}_{l}q_{m}=

\displaystyle-(\bar{q}\,\gamma_{5}T^{0}q)^{2}\,,

(425)

are obtained from linear combinations of
\mathcal{O}^{(S-P)_{+}}
in
Equation

414
,
\mathcal{O}^{(S+P)_{-}}
in
Equation

416
,
\mathcal{O}^{(S-P)_{-}}
in
Equation

418
, and
\mathcal{O}^{(S+P)_{+}}
in
Equation

420
.

Appendix C
Some flow functions

In this appendix we present explicit expressions for some flow equations. The threshold functions involved in this appendix can be found in, e.g.,
Fu et al. 2020a
;
Yin et al. 2019
.

The anomalous dimension of quarks
\eta_{q,k}
in
Equation

191
reads

\displaystyle\eta_{q,k}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{24\pi^{2}N_{f}}(4-\eta_{\phi,k})\bar{h}_{k}^{2}

\displaystyle\times\bigg\{(N_{f}^{2}-1)\mathcal{FB}_{(1,2)}(\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2},\tilde{m}_{\pi,k}^{2};T,\mu_{q},p_{0,\text{\tiny{ex}}})

\displaystyle+\mathcal{FB}_{(1,2)}(\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2},\tilde{m}_{\sigma,k}^{2};T,\mu_{q},p_{0,\text{\tiny{ex}}})\bigg\}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{24\pi^{2}}\frac{N_{c}^{2}-1}{2N_{c}}g_{\bar{q}Aq,k}^{2}

\displaystyle\times\bigg\{2(4-\eta_{A,k})\mathcal{FB}_{(1,2)}(\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2},0;T,\mu_{q},p_{0,\text{\tiny{ex}}})

\displaystyle+3(3-\eta_{q,k})\Big(\mathcal{FB}_{(1,1)}(\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2},0;T,\mu_{q},p_{0,\text{\tiny{ex}}})

\displaystyle-2\mathcal{FB}_{(2,1)}(\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2},0;T,\mu_{q},p_{0,\text{\tiny{ex}}})\Big)\bigg\},

(426)

with
N_{f}=2
and
N_{c}=3
. The anomalous dimension of mesons at
p=0
in
Equation

197
reads

\displaystyle\eta_{\phi,k}(0)=

\displaystyle\,\frac{\bar{Z}_{\phi,k}}{Z_{\phi,k}(0)}

\displaystyle\,\times\frac{1}{6\pi^{2}}\bigg\{\frac{4}{k^{2}}\bar{\kappa}_{k}(\bar{V}^{{}^{\prime\prime}}_{k}(\bar{\kappa}_{k}))^{2}\mathcal{BB}_{(2,2)}(\tilde{m}_{\pi,k}^{2},\tilde{m}_{\sigma,k}^{2};T)

\displaystyle\,+N_{c}\bar{h}_{k}^{2}\Big[(2\eta_{q,k}-3)\mathcal{F}_{(2)}(\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2};T,\mu_{q})

\displaystyle\,-4(\eta_{q,k}-2)\mathcal{F}_{(3)}(\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2};T,\mu_{q})\Big]\bigg\}\,,

(427)

and that at
p_{0}=0
and
\bm{p}^{2}=k^{2}
in
Equation

195
reads

\displaystyle\eta_{\phi,k}(0,k)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2}{3\pi^{2}}\frac{1}{k^{2}}\bar{\kappa}_{k}(\bar{V}^{{}^{\prime\prime}}_{k}(\bar{\kappa}_{k}))^{2}\mathcal{BB}_{(2,2)}(\tilde{m}_{\pi,k}^{2},\tilde{m}_{\sigma,k}^{2};T)

\displaystyle-\frac{N_{c}}{\pi^{2}}\bar{h}_{k}^{2}\int_{0}^{1}dx\bigg[(1-\eta_{q,k})\sqrt{x}+\eta_{q,k}x\bigg]

\displaystyle\times\int_{-1}^{1}d\cos\theta\Bigg\{\bigg[\Big(\mathcal{FF}_{(1,1)}(\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2},\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2})-\mathcal{F}_{(2)}(\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2})\Big)

\displaystyle-\Big(\mathcal{FF}_{(2,1)}(\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2},\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2})-\mathcal{F}_{(3)}(\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2})\Big)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\bigg[\Big(\sqrt{x}-\cos\theta\Big)\Big(1+r_{F}(x^{\prime})\Big)\mathcal{FF}_{(2,1)}(\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2},\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2})

\displaystyle-\mathcal{F}_{(3)}(\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2})\bigg]-\frac{1}{2}\bigg[\Big(\sqrt{x}-\cos\theta\Big)\Big(1+r_{F}(x^{\prime})\Big)

\displaystyle\times\mathcal{FF}_{(1,1)}(\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2},\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2})-\mathcal{F}_{(2)}(\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2})\bigg]\Bigg\}\,,

(428)

with
x=\bm{q}^{2}/k^{2}
and
x^{\prime}=(\bm{q}-\bm{p})^{2}/k^{2}
, where
\bm{q}
and
\bm{p}
stand for the loop and external 3-momenta, respectively, and
\theta
is the angle between them. The external momentum is chosen to be
|\bm{p}|=k
.

The contribution to the gluon anomalous dimension from the quark loop reads

\displaystyle\eta_{A}^{q}

\displaystyle=-\frac{N_{f}}{\pi^{2}}g_{\bar{q}Aq,k}^{2}\int_{0}^{1}dx\bigg[(1-\eta_{q,k})\sqrt{x}+\eta_{q,k}x\bigg]

\displaystyle\times\int_{-1}^{1}d\cos\theta\bigg[\Big(\mathcal{FF}_{(1,1)}(\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2},\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2})

\displaystyle-\mathcal{FF}_{(2,1)}(\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2},\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2})\Big)+\Big(\sqrt{x}\cos^{2}\!\theta-\cos\theta\Big)

\displaystyle\times\Big(1+r_{F}(x^{\prime})\Big)\Big(\mathcal{FF}_{(2,1)}(\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2},\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2})

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\mathcal{FF}_{(1,1)}(\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2},\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2})\Big)\bigg]\,.

(429)

As same as in
Equation

428
, the external momentum in
Equation

429
is chosen to be
|\bm{p}|=k
. The in-medium contribution from the light quarks, included in
Equation

200
, is given by

\displaystyle\Delta\eta_{A}^{q}

\displaystyle=\eta_{A}^{q}-\eta_{A}^{q}\big|_{T,\mu=0}\,,

(430)

with
\bar{m}_{q}=\bar{m}_{l}
,
N_{f}=2
and the light-quark–gluon coupling,
g_{\bar{l}Al}
in
Equation

429
. For the strange quark, since the vacuum contribution is also presented in
Equation

201
, one needs

\displaystyle\eta_{A}^{s}

\displaystyle=\eta^{s}_{A,\mathrm{vac}}+\Delta\eta_{A}^{s}\,,

(431)

which is obtained from
Equation

429
with
\bar{m}_{q}=\bar{m}_{s}
,
N_{f}=1
and the strange-quark–gluon coupling
g_{\bar{s}As}
.

The second term on the r.h.s. of
Equation

200
,
\Delta\eta_{A}^{\mathrm{glue}}
, denotes the contribution to the gluon anomalous dimension from the thermal part of glue sector, which is taken into account through the thermal screening mass of gluons. The modified gluon anomalous dimension reads

\displaystyle\bar{\eta}_{A}=

\displaystyle\eta_{A}+\frac{\Delta m_{\textrm{scr}}^{2}(k,T)}{\bar{Z}_{A}k^{2}}(2-\eta_{A})

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\bar{Z}_{A}k^{2}}\partial_{t}\big(\Delta m_{\textrm{scr}}^{2}(k,T)\big)\,.

(432)

with
\bar{\eta}_{A}=-\partial_{t}\bar{Z}_{A}/\bar{Z}_{A}
, where
\eta_{A}
is the gluon anomalous dimension without the thermal screening mass, and see
Fu et al. 2020a
for a more detailed discussion. The screening mass reads

\displaystyle\Delta m_{\textrm{scr}}^{2}(k,T)

\displaystyle=(c\,T)^{2}\exp\Big[-\Big(\frac{k}{\pi T}\Big)^{n}\Big]\,.

(433)

where
c=2
is adopted for
N_{f}=2+1
, which is consistent with the result in
Cyrol et al. 2018a
. Furthermore,
n=2
is chosen in
Equation

433
.

The flow of the quark-gluon couplings in
Equation

211
and
Equation

212
are given by

\displaystyle\overline{\textrm{Flow}}^{(3),A}_{(\bar{q}qA)}=

\displaystyle\frac{3}{8\pi^{2}N_{c}}\bar{g}_{\bar{q}qA,k}^{3}\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2}\bigg\{\frac{2}{15}(5-\eta_{A,k})\mathcal{FB}_{(2,2)}(\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2},0)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{3}(4-\eta_{q,k})\mathcal{FB}_{(3,1)}(\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2},0)\bigg\}

\displaystyle+\frac{3N_{c}}{8\pi^{2}}\bar{g}_{\bar{q}qA,k}^{2}\bar{g}_{A^{3},k}\bigg\{\frac{1}{20}(5-\eta_{q,k})\mathcal{FB}_{(1,2)}(\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2},0)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{6}(4-\eta_{q,k})\mathcal{FB}_{(2,1)}(\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2},0)+\frac{1}{30}(5-2\eta_{q,k})

\displaystyle\times\mathcal{FB}_{(2,2)}(\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2},0)-\frac{4}{15}(5-\eta_{A,k})

\displaystyle\times\mathcal{FB}_{(1,2)}(\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2},0)+\frac{1}{30}(10-3\eta_{A,k})

\displaystyle\times\mathcal{FB}_{(1,3)}(\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2},0)\bigg\}\,.

(434)

and

\displaystyle\overline{\textrm{Flow}}^{(3),\phi}_{{(\bar{q}qA)}}=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8\pi^{2}N_{f}}\bar{g}_{\bar{q}qA,k}\bar{h}_{k}^{2}\bigg\{\frac{1}{6}(4-\eta_{q,k})

\displaystyle\times\Big[\mathcal{FB}_{(2,1)}(\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2},\tilde{m}_{\sigma,k}^{2})

\displaystyle+2\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2}\mathcal{FB}_{(3,1)}(\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2},\tilde{m}_{\sigma,k}^{2})\Big]+\frac{2}{15}(5-\eta_{\phi,k})

\displaystyle\times\Big[\mathcal{FB}_{(1,2)}(\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2},\tilde{m}_{\sigma,k}^{2})

\displaystyle+\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2}\mathcal{FB}_{(2,2)}(\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2},\tilde{m}_{\sigma,k}^{2})\Big]\bigg\}

\displaystyle-\frac{N_{f}^{2}-1}{8\pi^{2}N_{f}}\bar{g}_{\bar{q}qA,k}\bar{h}_{k}^{2}\bigg\{\frac{1}{6}(4-\eta_{q,k})

\displaystyle\times\Big[\mathcal{FB}_{(2,1)}(\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2},\tilde{m}_{\pi,k}^{2})

\displaystyle+2\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2}\mathcal{FB}_{(3,1)}(\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2},\tilde{m}_{\pi,k}^{2})\Big]+\frac{2}{15}(5-\eta_{\phi,k})

\displaystyle\times\Big[\mathcal{FB}_{(1,2)}(\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2},\tilde{m}_{\pi,k}^{2})

\displaystyle+\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2}\mathcal{FB}_{(2,2)}(\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2},\tilde{m}_{\pi,k}^{2})\Big]\bigg\}\,.

(435)

Here for the light-quark–gluon coupling in
Equation

211
we use the light quark mass and
N_{f}=2
, and for the strange-quark–gluon coupling in
Equation

212
we use the strange quark mass and
N_{f}=1
.

The flow of the four-quark coupling in the
\sigma-\pi
channel from two gluon exchanges in
Equation

220
, as diagrammatically shown in the first line of
Figure

39
, reads

\displaystyle\overline{\mathrm{Flow}}^{(4),A}_{(\bar{q}\tau q)^{2}}

\displaystyle=-\frac{3}{2\pi^{2}}\frac{N_{c}^{2}-1}{2N_{c}}\Big(\frac{3}{4}-\frac{1}{N_{c}^{2}}\Big)\bar{g}_{\bar{q}Aq,k}^{4}

\displaystyle\quad\times\bigg\{\frac{2}{15}(5-\eta_{A,k})\Big[\mathcal{FB}_{(1,3)}(\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2},0)

\displaystyle\quad-\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2}\mathcal{FB}_{(2,3)}(\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2},0)\Big]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{12}(4-\eta_{q,k})\Big[\mathcal{FB}_{(2,2)}(\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2},0)

\displaystyle\quad-2\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2}\mathcal{FB}_{(3,2)}(\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2},0)\Big]\bigg\}\,.

(436)

The contributions to the flow of four-quark coupling from two meson exchanges, as shown in the second line of
Figure

39
, reads

\displaystyle\overline{\mathrm{Flow}}^{(4),\phi}_{(\bar{q}\tau q)^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{32\pi^{2}}\frac{N_{f}^{2}-2}{N_{f}N_{c}}\bar{h}_{k}^{4}\bigg\{\frac{2}{15}(5-\eta_{\phi,k})

\displaystyle\quad\times\Big[\Big(\mathcal{FBB}_{(1,1,2)}(\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2},\tilde{m}_{\pi,k}^{2},\tilde{m}_{\sigma,k}^{2})

\displaystyle\quad+\mathcal{FBB}_{(1,1,2)}(\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2},\tilde{m}_{\sigma,k}^{2},\tilde{m}_{\pi,k}^{2})

\displaystyle\quad-2\mathcal{FB}_{(1,3)}(\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2},\tilde{m}_{\pi,k}^{2})\Big)

\displaystyle\quad-\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2}\Big(\mathcal{FBB}_{(2,1,2)}(\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2},\tilde{m}_{\pi,k}^{2},\tilde{m}_{\sigma,k}^{2})

\displaystyle\quad+\mathcal{FBB}_{(2,2,1)}(\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2},\tilde{m}_{\pi,k}^{2},\tilde{m}_{\sigma,k}^{2})

\displaystyle\quad-2\mathcal{FB}_{(2,3)}(\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2},\tilde{m}_{\pi,k}^{2})\Big)\Big]+\frac{1}{6}(4-\eta_{q,k})

\displaystyle\quad\times\Big[\Big(\mathcal{FBB}_{(2,1,1)}(\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2},\tilde{m}_{\pi,k}^{2},\tilde{m}_{\sigma,k}^{2})

\displaystyle\quad-\mathcal{FB}_{(2,2)}(\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2},\tilde{m}_{\pi,k}^{2})\Big)

\displaystyle\quad-2\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2}\Big(\mathcal{FBB}_{(3,1,1)}(\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2},\tilde{m}_{\pi,k}^{2},\tilde{m}_{\sigma,k}^{2})

\displaystyle\quad-\mathcal{FB}_{(3,2)}(\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2},\tilde{m}_{\pi,k}^{2})\Big)\Big]\bigg\}\,.

(437)

The flow of the Yukawa coupling in
Equation

225
reads

\displaystyle\overline{\mathrm{Flow}}^{(3)}_{(\bar{q}\bm{\tau}q)\bm{\pi}}

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{1}{4\pi^{2}N_{f}}\bar{h}_{k}^{3}\bigg[-(N_{f}^{2}-1)

\displaystyle\qquad\times L_{(1,1)}^{(4)}(\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2},\tilde{m}_{\pi,k}^{2},\eta_{q,k},\eta_{\phi,k};T,\mu_{q},p_{0,\text{\tiny{ex}}})

\displaystyle\qquad+L_{(1,1)}^{(4)}(\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2},\tilde{m}_{\sigma,k}^{2},\eta_{q,k},\eta_{\phi,k};T,\mu_{q},p_{0,\text{\tiny{ex}}})\bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{3}{2\pi^{2}}\frac{N_{c}^{2}-1}{2N_{c}}\bar{g}_{\bar{q}qA,k}^{2}\bar{h}_{k}

\displaystyle\qquad\times L_{(1,1)}^{(4)}(\tilde{m}_{q,k}^{2},0,\eta_{q,k},\eta_{A,k};T,\mu_{q},p_{0,\text{\tiny{ex}}})\,.

(438)
